\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left(E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1})\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}\right)\to_{P}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left(E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})E(\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top})\right).

We will prove this convergence result by showing

\left\|\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left(\left[E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1})-E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})\right]\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}\right)\right\|_{1}=o(1)

and

\left\|\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left(E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})\left[\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}-E(\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top})\right]\right)\right\|_{1}=o(1),

respectively.

Consider
\left\|\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left(\left[E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1})-E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})\right]\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}\right)\right\|_{1}
.

Let
\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}=E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1})-E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})
and thus
E(\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*})=0
. For any integer
I\geq 1
introduce the truncated process
\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}=E\left(\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}|\mathcal{F}_{t-I}\right)
. Then
\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}=0
if
t\leq I
and
\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}=\sum_{s=1}^{t-I}w_{s,t}\mathbf{y}_{s}
for
1\leq I<t
. For
2\leq t\leq T
, by using Burkholder inequality again,

\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}-\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\|_{\delta}^{2}\leq M\max_{t}\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\|_{\delta}^{2}\sum_{s=\max(1,t-I+1)}^{t-1}w_{s,t}^{2}=O\left(\sum_{s=\max(1,t-I+1)}^{t-1}w_{s,t}^{2}\right).

Let
L(I)=\sum_{J=1}^{I}l(J)
with
l(J)=\sum_{s=1}^{T-J}w_{s,s+J}^{2}
,
V(I)=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}\right]
and

T(I)=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[E(\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}|\mathcal{F}_{t-I})\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}\right].

Note that for any fixed integer
J
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{T-J}w_{s,s+J}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{T-J}\frac{1}{T^{2}h}\left[\int_{-1}^{1}K\left(u\right)K\left(u+\frac{J}{Th}\right)\mathrm{d}u\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{T-J}{T^{2}h}\left[\int_{-1}^{1}K\left(u\right)K\left(u+\frac{J}{Th}\right)\mathrm{d}u\right]^{2}=O(1/(Th)).

In addition, by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, if
I/(Th)\to 0
, we have

\displaystyle\|V(1)-V(I)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\left(\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}-\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}\right)\right]\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}-\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\|_{4}\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}+\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}-\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\|_{4}a_{t}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\left\{\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}-\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\|_{4}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\sum_{t=2}^{T}a_{t}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1)[L(I)]^{1/2}\to 0,

since

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}+\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{M\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\|w_{s,t}\mathbf{y}_{s}\|_{4}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}+\left\{M\sum_{s=1}^{t-I}\|w_{s,t}\mathbf{y}_{s}\|_{4}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\left\{\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}w_{s,t}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\right)=O(a_{t}^{1/2}).

Define the projection operator
\mathcal{P}_{t}\bm{\xi}=E\left(\bm{\xi}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t}\right)-E\left(\bm{\xi}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right)
. For
0\leq j\leq I-1
, let
U(j)=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathcal{P}_{t-j}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\right)\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}\right]
, then

V(I)-T(I)=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\sum_{j=0}^{I-1}\mathcal{P}_{t-j}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\right)\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}\right]=\sum_{j=0}^{I-1}U(j).

Note that
\left\{\left(\mathcal{P}_{t-j}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\right)\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}\right\}_{t=2}^{T}
forms a martingale difference sequence since

E\left\{\left(\mathcal{P}_{t-j}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\right)\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}|\mathcal{F}_{t-j-1}\right\}=\left[E(\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}|\mathcal{F}_{t-j-1})-E(\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}|\mathcal{F}_{t-j-1})\right]\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}=0.

By Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, since
\|\mathcal{P}_{t-j}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\|_{\delta/2}\leq 2\|\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\|_{\delta/2}<\infty
,

\displaystyle\|U(j)\|_{\delta/4}^{\delta/4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\left(\mathcal{P}_{t-j}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\right)\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*,\top}\|_{\delta/4}^{\delta/4}\leq M\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\|_{\delta}^{\delta/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\sum_{t=2}^{T}a_{t}^{\delta/4}\leq M\max_{t}a_{t}^{\delta/4-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}a_{t}=O\left(T^{1-\delta/4}\right).

In addition, by
\|V(1)-V(I)\|_{1}\to 0
,

\displaystyle\|V(1)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|V(I)-T(I)\|_{\delta/4}+E|T(I)|+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{I-1}\|U(j)\|_{\delta/4}+\max_{t}\|E(\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}|\mathcal{F}_{t-I})\|_{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\mathbf{h}_{t-1,I}^{*}\|_{4}^{2}=o(1),

since
\max_{t}\|E(\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}|\mathcal{F}_{t-I})\|_{2}\to 0
as
I\to\infty
. We then have proved

\left\|\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left(\left[E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t-1})-E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})\right]\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}\right)\right\|_{1}=o(1).

Consider
\left\|\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left(E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})\left[\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}-E(\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top})\right]\right)\right\|_{1}
. For notational simplicity, we omit
E(\mathbf{H}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*,\top}\mathbf{H}_{t})
and write

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}-E\left(\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*}\mathbf{h}_{t-1}^{*,\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{s-1}\mathbf{u}_{s}\mathbf{u}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}-E\left(\mathbf{W}_{s-1}\mathbf{u}_{s}\mathbf{u}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\right)\right)w_{s,t}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\sum_{t=3}^{T}\sum_{s_{1}=2}^{t-1}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{s_{1}-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathbf{W}_{s_{1}-1}\mathbf{u}_{s_{1}}\mathbf{u}_{s_{2}}\mathbf{W}_{s_{2}-1}^{\top}w_{s_{1},t}w_{s_{2},t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|K_{T,24}|+2|K_{T,25}|.

Consider
|K_{T,24}|
. Write

\displaystyle K_{T,24}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{s}\mathbf{u}_{s}^{\top}-\bm{\Omega}(\tau_{s})\right)\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}\right]w_{s,t}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\bm{\Omega}(\tau_{s})\left(\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}-E\left(\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}\right)\right)\right]w_{s,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{s}\mathbf{u}_{s}^{\top}-\bm{\Omega}(\tau_{s})\right)\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}\right]\left(T\sum_{t=s+1}^{T}w_{s,t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\mathrm{tr}\left[\bm{\Omega}(\tau_{s})\left(\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}-E\left(\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}\right)\right)\right]\left(T\sum_{t=s+1}^{T}w_{s,t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle K_{T,24,1}+K_{T,24,2}.

Since
\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\mathbf{u}_{s}\mathbf{u}_{s}^{\top}-\bm{\Omega}(\tau_{s})\right)\mathbf{W}_{s-1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{s-1}\right]
,
s=1,2,\ldots
are a martingale difference sequence and
T\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{s,t}^{2}=O(1)
, we have
K_{T,24,1}=o_{P}(1)
. In addition, similarly to the proof of Lemma
A.1
(the convergence of the estimator of the sample covariance of time-varying VMA(
\infty
) processes), we have
K_{T,24,2}=o_{P}(1)
.

Next, consider
K_{T,25}
. By Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and Burkholder inequality,

\displaystyle\|K_{T,25}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\sum_{s_{1}=2}^{T-1}\|\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathbf{W}_{s_{1}-1}\bm{\eta}_{s_{1}}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{s_{1}-1}\mathbf{u}_{s_{2}}\mathbf{W}_{s_{2}-1}^{\top}\sum_{t=s_{1}+1}^{T}w_{s_{1},t}w_{s_{2},t}\right]\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\sum_{s_{1}=2}^{T-1}\|\mathbf{W}_{s_{1}-1}\mathbf{u}_{s_{1}}\|_{4}^{2}\|\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{s_{1}-1}\mathbf{W}_{s_{2}-1}\mathbf{u}_{s_{2}}\sum_{t=s_{1}+1}^{T}w_{s_{1},t}w_{s_{2},t}\|_{4}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\sum_{s_{1}=2}^{T-1}\|\mathbf{W}_{s_{1}-1}\mathbf{u}_{s_{1}}\|_{4}^{2}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{s_{1}-1}\|\mathbf{W}_{s_{2}-1}\mathbf{u}_{s_{2}}\sum_{t=s_{1}+1}^{T}w_{s_{1},t}w_{s_{2},t}\|_{4}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\sum_{s_{1}=2}^{T-1}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{s_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{t=s_{1}+1}^{T}w_{s_{1},t}w_{s_{2},t}\right)^{2}\right)=O(1/T)

provided that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{T-1}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\left[\sum_{j=k+1}^{T}w_{k,j}w_{t,j}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{4}h^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{T-1}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\left(\sum_{j=1+k}^{T}\left[\int_{-1}^{1}K\left(u\right)K\left(u+\frac{j-k}{Th}\right)\mathrm{d}u\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{j=1+k}^{T}\left[\int_{-1}^{1}K\left(u\right)K\left(u+\frac{j-t}{Th}\right)\mathrm{d}u\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{M}{T^{3}h}\sum_{k=1}^{T-1}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\sum_{j=1+k}^{T}\left[\int_{-1}^{1}K\left(u\right)K\left(u+\frac{j-t}{Th}\right)\mathrm{d}u\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{M}{T^{3}h}\sum_{k=1}^{T-1}\sum_{j=2}^{T}\sum_{t=1}^{j-1}\left[\int_{-1}^{1}K\left(u\right)K\left(u+\frac{j-t}{Th}\right)\mathrm{d}u\right]^{2}=O(1/T).

Combining the above results, the proof is now completed.
∎
